([trozen/tpy-lang](https://github.com/trozen/tpy-lang/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tpy-review.convention-compliance.md) · ★26)
- **convention-compliance**: Reviews a change against the project's OWN written conventions — the rules in AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, .editorconfig and lint configs — rather than against general good practice. Every finding must quote the exact rule text it claims is violated, and a rule you cannot quote verbatim is a rule you may not report. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/agents/convention-compliance.md) · ★2)
- **convention-compliance-reviewer**: Review diffs against universal project-kit conventions — conventional-commits format, the no-shared-files invariant, branch-naming, surface-change discipline. Flags violations without fixing them; surfaces actionable diagnoses for the author. ([aleskalfas/project-kit](https://github.com/aleskalfas/project-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/convention-compliance-reviewer.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **convention-compliance-reviewer**: Reviews a git diff against the project's declared CODE conventions (design tokens, naming, file-size limits, state-management contract, accessibility, test expectations). ([haimlevtov/Samson](https://github.com/haimlevtov/Samson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/convention-compliance-reviewer.md))
- **convention-conformance-reviewer**: You are the **Convention Conformance & Code Reuse** reviewer in a staff-level code review. Your bias is simplicity and anti-bloat: the most valuable finding you can make is "this reimplements something that already exists." Read the diff and the Research Brief, then check whether the change follows the repository's conventions and reuses existing code instead of rebuilding it. ([smykla-skalski/sai](https://github.com/smykla-skalski/sai/blob/HEAD/plugins/staff-code-review/skills/staff-code-review/references/agents/convention-conformance-reviewer.md) · ★5)

- **conventions**: - A parameter noun before a measurement (`при температуре 660 °C`, `со скоростью 60 км/ч`, `диаметром 5 м`) is named by that measurement, so the measurement is an apposition and stays nominative in any case the parameter itself carries. The genitive reading needs an explicit `в` in the source: `при температуре в 660 градусов`. - **conventions**: Review the PR diff for deviations from this codebase's own established conventions and idioms. This is the "strong teammate" lens: the suggestions a senior engineer on this team would leave: non-blocking, but they make the code match how the rest of the codebase is written. You are the ONLY agent allowed to look beyond the diff for evidence; the other agents are diff-scoped, you are not. ([oleg-koval/agent-skills](https://github.com/oleg-koval/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/olko-github-pr/skills/lekker-review/references/agents/conventions.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **CONVENTIONS**: Use this when the developer chose to review their conventions up front during setup ([SETUP.md](SETUP.md), step 7), or asks for it later. You and the developer work them out together. At the end they are in the project's docs, in the developer's words, and [DRAFT-RULES.md](DRAFT-RULES.md) can turn them into rules. ([syv-ai/rulecast](https://github.com/syv-ai/rulecast/blob/HEAD/agents/CONVENTIONS.md) · ★3)

Write a word in capitals only ([Banon-Labs/er-mods-rs](https://github.com/Banon-Labs/er-mods-rs/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★2)
- **conventions**: Canonical, deterministic rulebook. `CLAUDE.md` and `AGENTS.md` — root and `payload/` alike — point here instead of copying it; this is the single source. - **conventions**: The codebase's house rules. These are **enforced** by `pnpm verify` (where mechanical) and by code review (where not). Read this before making non-trivial changes. ([abapify/adt-bench](https://github.com/abapify/adt-bench/blob/HEAD/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)

- **conventions**: The rules that hold on every turn: contexture's baseline, then this store's own. ([ayxuerui/contexture](https://github.com/ayxuerui/contexture/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
- **conventions**: `eslint.config.js` sets `'@semi': ['error', 'never']`, mirroring `ij_typescript_force_semicolon_style = false` in `.editorconfig`. ([DarCas/refero-design-mcp](https://github.com/DarCas/refero-design-mcp/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
- **conventions**: **World space** (installation frame): **X=right, Y=forward, Z=up, centimetres** — right-handed. ([Impractical-Instruments/CommunityGarden](https://github.com/Impractical-Instruments/CommunityGarden/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
- **conventions**: Tactical rules that are load-bearing but don't rise to an ADR: if you break one, something quietly stops working or a future change gets harder. ADRs (`docs/adr/`) hold the decisions with alternatives and consequences; `CONTEXT.md` holds the domain language; this file holds the "when you touch X, also do Y" knowledge that has nowhere else to live. ([kerti/balances-v2](https://github.com/kerti/balances-v2/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
- **conventions**: Aprende o padrão da casa e escreve os arquivos. Use proactively na primeira tarefa de uma codebase, em paralelo com recon e planner. Não implementa a tarefa. ([mauriciosm13/universal-api-gateway](https://github.com/mauriciosm13/universal-api-gateway/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
- **conventions**: - Use `internal/` for tool-specific, `pkg/` for shared code - Error handling: `fmt.Errorf("context: %w", err)` - Logging: `log/slog` with structured fields - No external dependencies for core functionality - SQLite: read-only connections to external DBs - Run tests with `-race` flag ([mistakeknot/Autarch](https://github.com/mistakeknot/Autarch/blob/HEAD/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
- **conventions**: - 中文为主，代码/术语保留英文。 - 子文档**主题语义命名**（`architecture.md`、`data-model.md`），**不用数字序号前缀**。 - 标题用 Markdown 天然层级语义式命名，不加 `## 1. X` 这类数字前缀。 - `docs/` 是给人看的解释性文档，`skills/*/SKILL.md` 是给模型看的操作指令，两者分工不重叠。 ([morehao/agent-skills](https://github.com/morehao/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
- **conventions**: How code and prose are written here. Match the surrounding file; when in doubt, this document decides. ([schematron-rust/schematron-rust](https://github.com/schematron-rust/schematron-rust/blob/HEAD/schematron/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)

- **conventions**: - Comments: lean, present-tense, only for constraints the code cannot show. ([tviles/skillify](https://github.com/tviles/skillify/blob/HEAD/agents/conversation-analyzer.md))

Use when reviewing session transcripts for patterns of mistakes or corrections. ([klu-dev/porting-ecc-to-vscode](https://github.com/klu-dev/porting-ecc-to-vscode/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/conversation-analyzer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **conversation-analyzer.agent**: Conversation analysis for hookify — scan user messages for frustration signals, corrections, repeated issues, explicit "don't do X" requests. Detects unwanted tool behaviors and extracts regex patterns for hook rule generation. Triggered by hookify-create without arguments or explicit conversation analysis requests. ([Poorgramer-Zack/copilot-cli-things](https://github.com/Poorgramer-Zack/copilot-cli-things/blob/HEAD/plugins/hookify/agents/conversation-analyzer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **conversation-anchor**: 会话锚点（Conversation Anchor）是 Runtime 为 LocalAgent 的持久 Conversation 签发的连续性标识，帮助已授权界面接续同一段对话。它不是访问凭证，也不能替代当前会话的 `agentHandle`。 ([nimiplatform/nimi](https://github.com/nimiplatform/nimi/blob/HEAD/docs/zh/platform/agents/conversation-anchor.md) · ★5)
- **conversation-anchor**: A Conversation Anchor is a Runtime-issued continuity token for a LocalAgent’s durable Conversation. It helps authorized surfaces refer to the same conversation; it is not an authorization credential or a replacement for the current session’s `agentHandle`. ([nimiplatform/nimi](https://github.com/nimiplatform/nimi/blob/HEAD/docs/platform/agents/conversation-anchor.md) · ★5)
- **conversation-architect**: Owns the Ollama HTTP client, the multi-turn context window, and the runtime assembly of (system prompt + history + new user message) into a single LLM call. Scope SHRUNK in 2026-06-06: prompt content moved to prompt-engineer; DB schema and writes moved to db-steward. Invoke when changing the Ollama endpoint, timeout, retry logic, context-window eviction rules, or session-timeout logic. Touches src/services/ollamaService.js and src/services/conversationManager.js only. ([hochoa13/whatsapp-agent-harlest](https://github.com/hochoa13/whatsapp-agent-harlest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/conversation-architect.md) · ★1)
- **conversation-assessor**: Reads one exported batch of conversations and writes one verdict file inside work/results/. Use it only for `tracker ai export` batches; it is given a file path and answers with a file path. It never runs commands and never reaches the network. ([roccoterr97/threadline](https://github.com/roccoterr97/threadline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/conversation-assessor.md) · ★1)
- **conversation-chunk-analyzer**: Analyze a single chunk of a long conversation, producing a deeply granular 8-section analysis with exact quotes, behavioral observations, and emotional arc tracking. Used by the conversation-summary skill to process chunks in parallel. ([ylt/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ylt/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/joes-toolkit/agents/conversation-chunk-analyzer.md))

- **conversation-compacter**: Compacts long conversations into ~300 token summaries preserving critical context, todos, file paths, and decisions. Use when approaching 150k+ tokens. - **cook**: Cook (Chef de Partie) — the brigade's implementer. Works a station the chef assigns (a disjoint set of files) and cooks it with TDD. Stack- and layer-agnostic: backend, frontend, data, infra, docs — whatever the station is. The chef runs several cooks in parallel on non-overlapping lanes. Lean merge of the muppets' scooter + pepe-the-king-prawn into one generic role. ([avegancafe/kitchen-brigade](https://github.com/avegancafe/kitchen-brigade/blob/HEAD/agents/cook.md))

- **cook-audit**: jeff `audit` stage (conditional: runs when the plan flags a security-relevant surface, or when the mechanical scan floor forces it). Adversarial security audit of the task's change, scanner-first. Verdict pass / needs-work / na; every finding self-classified blocking or follow-up. Do not edit code. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-audit.md) · ★6)
- **cook-council**: jeff `council` inquiry station. Independently investigate the exact active blocker union through one assigned lens. Read only; do not synthesize, vote for another member, or edit. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-council.md) · ★6)
- **cook-council-synthesis**: jeff council synthesis station. Independently synthesize three validated inquiries, compare materially different strategies, and select the bounded recovery route. Read only; do not edit or execute. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-council-synthesis.md) · ★6)
- **cook-design**: Cook spawné sur tout ticket qui touche la vue de l'app (`app/src/vue/`) — impose la langue visuelle de l'app et la boucle capture avant → changement → capture après → correction. ([benomite/brigade](https://github.com/benomite/brigade/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-design.md) · ★3)
- **cook-execute**: jeff `execute` stage for operation tasks. Perform the bounded state transition, preserve the recovery boundary, and record actions and evidence. Stop before an exact irreversible shared mutation until the operator approves it. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-execute.md) · ★6)
- **cook-implement**: jeff `implement` stage. Make the failing tests green with the smallest correct change. May NOT author or weaken the tests: those came from a separate agent and must stay intact. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-implement.md) · ★6)
- **cook-plan**: jeff `plan` stage. For code tasks, design and author the test contract and prove RED. For operation tasks, define the bounded runbook and deterministic verification contract. Never implement. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-plan.md) · ★6)
- **cook-refactor**: jeff `refactor` stage. Simplify a green implementation without changing behavior, or perform a council-selected direct recovery refactor that changes behavior. Tests must stay green. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-refactor.md) · ★6)
- **cook-refute**: jeff `refute` pass. Adversarially test exactly one blocking review, verification, or audit finding before it triggers a kickback. Verdict survives or refuted, with evidence. Do not edit. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-refute.md) · ★6)
- **cook-review**: jeff `review` stage. Independently review the task's change against its acceptance criteria and the Chef's standards. You did not write this code. Verdict is pass or needs-work; every finding self-classified blocking or follow-up. Do not edit code. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-review.md) · ★6)
- **cook-verify**: jeff `verify` stage for operation tasks. Independently verify every deterministic postcondition and acceptance criterion after execution. Verdict pass or needs-work; do not edit state. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-verify.md) · ★6)

- **cookbook-engine**: `@happyvertical/smrt-core/cookbook/engine` (`src/cookbook/engine/`) is the one place the rules for changing a [cookbook](cookbook.md) live. A planner UI, a local Kitchen MCP server, the CLI and a website all call it; each is an adapter. It imports only relative modules and `@happyvertical/smrt-types` types, so it runs in Node and in a browser. No SvelteKit, storage, singleton or inference. Claude has built-ins absent; native Codex 0.162.1 now enables its necessary code-mode host and confines incidental patch/command items in disposable workspaces (inference's engine contract). Typical triggers include "is there an example for building RAG over X", "find a cookbook for this extraction use case", "what notebooks cover agentic workflows", and "show me a sample close to what I'm building".
